Folding the two readings would have refused a discard this card does not refuse. **⚠️ THE FOUR STRUCTURAL QUESTIONS.** **(1) PARK?** No — `preventDamage` is deterministic and this producer is UNGATED, so the program is a single non-parking op and cannot reach `EffectContinuation`. **`MATCH_RECORD_VERSION` STAYS 5, for a THIRD distinct reason**: the field lands INSIDE `AttackBlock` (a record that only exists when a block was installed) rather than on `InPlayPokemon`, it is OPTIONAL, and an old `{ turn, effects }` is still a valid inhabitant of the new type reading back as "refuses every attacker" — which is what it meant. Contrast D142/D143, which bumped for a REQUIRED field on `InPlayPokemon` itself. **(2) VOICE?** The install row gains `fromClass` and a THIRD phrasing (*"… is protected from damage from Basic Pokémon's attacks during your opponent's next turn"*), for `effects`' reason made sharper — a filtered block lets every evolved attacker through at FULL damage, so the old wording would be an outright lie about the very next turn. **And a block that FAILS to match emits NOTHING**: the attack's own `DAMAGE_DEALT` (`prevented` absent, the real number in `dealt`) is the whole story, and announcing a rule that declined would be announcing a non-event — loudness is owed to UNREAD TEXT, not to a read rule saying no (D140). **(3) GUARDS ASSERTED?** Three ways: the vocabulary is a COMPILER fact (the exhaustive switch), the PRODUCER SET is swept out of the pool rather than listed (D145's move — a third producer fails loudly), and the effects gate — unreachable from any printing, since all three filtered printings are the narrow spelling — honours the filter and is pinned by a CONSTRUCTED program (D144's precedent). **(4) DOES THE MECHANISM EXIST?** Read first, and yes; what did not exist is the DATUM for the second class token. Cost: 2 new fixtures (Staraptor sv01-150, Noivern ex sv02-153 — **each other's negative case**, a Stage 2 and a Stage 1, so "the filter failed" is driven off printed cards rather than a bespoke fixture), 1 new deck (`CLASS_BLOCK_DECK`), `classBlock.test.ts` (**27 tests, SEED-FREE** — the family's first UNGATED producer, pinned by an unchanged `rngState` across a whole install, D143's move), **1 re-pointed stays-LOUD witness, none deleted** (`preventBlock.test.ts`, NULL → SHAPE) and **1 added LIVE unmapped witness** (Noivern ex's index-1 "Dominating Echo", driven end to end to `ATTACK_EFFECT_SKIPPED` — D135's move). `clauseApostrophe`'s DISCOVERED census moved **41 → 42**. **Mutation-checked eight ways: dropping `fromClass` from the deriver fails 8, inverting the predicate fails 7, ignoring the field in `attackBlockOf` fails 2, dropping the anchor's `^` fails 1, ignoring the filter at the effects gate fails 1, keeping the filter on a merge disagreement fails 1, dropping the class from the install row fails 1, dropping it from the log arm fails 1.** engineVersion 0.94.0 → **0.95.0**; full `bun run check` **GREEN exit 0, 188 files / 3106 tests** (was 187 / 3079). |
| D147 | The DURATED DAMAGE REDUCTION — *"During your opponent's next turn, this Pokémon takes {20\|30\|50} less damage from attacks (after applying Weakness and Resistance)."* (12 printings) plus its COMPOUND twin *"Discard all Energy from this Pokémon. …100 less…"* (2) on **TWO anchored regexes, TWO deriver arms, ONE new op (`reduceDamage`), ONE new required `InPlayPokemon` field (`damageReduction`), ONE new `continuous.ts` reader and FOUR one-token read sites** — the LARGEST un-taken count in the P3-M5 tail (14 printings / 4 amounts), taken as item 1 of D146's remainder list. **⚠️ AND THE HALF THAT IS NOT A READER: THE `attackBlock` GENERALISATION, DEFERRED HERE BY D143 AND RE-DERIVED RATHER THAN INHERITED — VERDICT: A THIRD PARALLEL FIELD, WITH A NEW TRIGGER NAMED.** D143 parked the call on a precise premise: this would be the first reading to genuinely SHARE a read path with `attackBlock` (the same four damage sites, one line below it), *"which is the thing a union pays for"*, and D146 moved one input in the generalisation's favour by giving those sites a uniform `(state, pokemon, attacker)` signature. **The premise did not survive contact.** It shares the SITES and not the QUESTION: at each of the four sites the engine asks two independent things at two different steps of §8.5 — the reduction is SUBTRACTED inside the pipeline, the block SUPERSEDES its result — and D146's own filter makes the independence OBSERVABLE rather than notional, since a `fromClass` block can DECLINE an attacker while the reduction on the same body still bites (driven, not argued). **A discriminated union merges STORAGE, not lookups.** Priced concretely against D143's own table: it BUYS the three §10 clear sites ceasing to be hand-written literals (~3 lines, and the hazard they carry is already covered by D143's own swept clear-set test) and ONE bump instead of one per field (this slice bumps either way, so the saving is entirely in the FUTURE); it COSTS a discriminated union, a `find`-by-kind at every read site where today each site names its field and the compiler checks it, a harder install/merge (remove-then-push instead of one assignment), and a mechanical rewrite of every suite that surgeries a stamp. **If it had been taken, MRV would bump once more and then further durated effects would cost none — stated explicitly, because that is the real argument and it is a FUTURE saving weighed against a PRESENT and PERMANENT cost at the read sites.** **⚠️ THE FACT THAT ACTUALLY DECIDED IT: THIS WAS NEVER A NEW READING.** The pool prints this exact sentence MINUS its four-word duration prefix as an ALWAYS-ON ABILITY on **4 printings** — Bouffalant sv03-174 "Bouffer" (20), Stonjourner sv01-121 "Exoskeleton" (20), Copperajah ex sv02-150/-245 "Bronze Body" (30) — all simulated since 0.x as `PassiveEffects.damageReductionAfterWR`, aggregated by `passivesOf` and read at exactly these four sites with exactly this clamp. So the new field is a **SOURCE summed into a number the engine already computes**, not a second channel: the read-site diff is one `+` each, and the clamp, the `ignoreWR` bypass (Umbreon "Feint Attack" — an installed reduction IS "an effect on that Pokémon") and `DAMAGE_DEALT.reduction`'s reporting all come for free. That is D131's one-reading-one-implementation rule applied across the CATALOG/STATE boundary rather than within one side of it. **⚠️ THE NEW TRIGGER, NAMED SO THE NEXT DURATED SLICE INHERITS A TEST AND NOT A JUDGEMENT: the first caller that must ENUMERATE the installed effects on a body instead of asking for one by name.** Field-by-field storage answers *"is effect X live?"* perfectly and answers *"what is on this Pokémon?"* only with a hand-written list — which is the literal-list hazard a union removes. (b) a redact/HUD projection that must SHOW the durated riders as a list (today none crosses the wire — `RedactedConditions`/`BattleConditions` pin the shape field by field, and D143 folded its lock into an existing `playable` boolean);
